Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a electrical_engineering algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 648. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 525/5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to electrical_engineering theory, we decompose the problem into 648 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 525. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
